Spawns independent quality and integrity verifiers that never saw the build, collects their verdicts to disk, and computes consensus by script. ([Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins](https://github.com/Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/gauntlet/skills/gauntlet-verify) · ★2)
- **gauntlet-verify**: Use when the user explicitly invokes $gauntlet-loop:gauntlet-verify to evaluate a project with fresh independent judges, acceptance-to-evidence traceability, adversarial checks, and a bounded final verdict. Never use implicitly. ([Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins](https://github.com/Israelmusondaayliffe/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/gauntlet-loop/skills/gauntlet-verify) · ★2)
- **gauntlex:compare**: Diff two GAUNTLEX Resilience Reports — shows ARS delta, attack category shifts, new CWE coverage, and whether the codebase is improving or regressing. Returns exit 1 if ARS regression &gt; 0.05. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/compare) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:doctor**: Full GAUNTLEX environment health check — model reachable, ChromaDB writable, AVF fixtures pass, no unexpected outbound network (air-gap verification). Use before first run or when debugging failures. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/doctor) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:learn**: Ingest a completed GAUNTLEX run into the Knowledge Forge and update the Adaptive Brain's attack effectiveness scores. Call after reviewing a completed run to reinforce learning. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/learn) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:report**: Render a stored GAUNTLEX Resilience Report as Markdown, HTML, or structured JSON. Can attach to a GitHub PR comment. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/report) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:run**: Run a full GAUNTLEX adversarial session on a spec file or GitHub issue URL. Invokes the Gauntlex (Builder + Breaker concurrent) and outputs an Adversarial Resilience Score (ARS) with a Resilience Report. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/run) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:validate**: Dry-run GAUNTLEX validation — parses the spec, resolves policy playbooks, checks model availability, and runs the AVF golden fixture gate. No attacks are fired. Exit 1 if any check fails. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/validate) · ★1)
- **gauntlex:verify**: Re-derive the SHA-256 integrity hash over a Resilience Report's attack array and confirm it matches the stored hash. Detects tampered reports. ([sanjoy1234/gauntlex](https://github.com/sanjoy1234/gauntlex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gauntlex/verify) · ★1)
- **gauss-laguerre-sld-mixed-geometry**: Use when computing mixed-state QFIM/QGT or SLD with quantum algorithms, or benchmarking quantum metrology resource estimates. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/physics-math/gauss-laguerre-sld-mixed-geometry) · ★4)
- **gauss-legendre-angular-quadrature-transfer**: Use when a central point source ionizes a spherical hydrogen cloud and the analysis requires the effect of diffuse recombination photons rather than the on-the-spot approximation, e.g. reproducing the Raičević et al. (2014) Test 1 sphere. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gauss-legendre-angular-quadrature-transfer))
- **gauss-open-gauss-cli**: Open Gauss in this workspace is the math-inc/OpenGauss gauss CLI for Lean session tooling—not Huawei OpenGauss DBMS. See docs/reference/opengauss-naming.md; for the public Lean exemplar see projects/templates/template_active_inference/lean/. ([docxology/template](https://github.com/docxology/template/tree/HEAD/.agents/workflows/gauss) · ★24)
- **gauss-perspective**: 高斯的思维框架与数学直觉。基于高斯全集及学术传记的深度研究， 提炼数论王子、多领域开创、数即是宇宙秩序的核心心智模型。 用途：作为思维顾问，用高斯的视角分析数学问题、审视跨领域关联、提供审美判断。 当用户提到「用高斯的视角」「高斯会怎么看」「gauss perspective」时使用。 ([YintaTriss/Agent-superthinking](https://github.com/YintaTriss/Agent-superthinking/tree/HEAD/experts/experts/math/gauss-perspective) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **gauss-s-law**: Gauss's Law in Electromagnetism (Electrostatics). Theoretical foundations, governing equations, analytical problem-solving methods, and typical archetypes. ([hamzabellouch/agent-skills](https://github.com/hamzabellouch/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Physics/Electromagnetism_and_Electrodynamics/gauss-s-law) · ★8)
- **GaussDB Expert Engineer**: Expert database specialist focusing on GaussDB OLTP — Huawei's self-developed enterprise-grade relational database (NOT GaussDB(DWS) OLAP, NOT GaussDB(for openGauss) cloud service, NOT GaussDB(for MySQL)). Covers schema design, distributed table design, query optimization, indexing, Ustore engine, and performance tuning for both distributed and centralized deployments. ([aakash1552005/universal-agent-skills](https://github.com/aakash1552005/universal-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/engineering-gaussdb-expert) · ★4 · 4 copies)
- **gaussdb-adaptation**: Use this skill when porting SQL Server or vanilla PostgreSQL code to Huawei GaussDB (Kernel 505.x / openGauss-based). TRIGGER when source code or SQL contains any of — `Microsoft.Data.SqlClient`, `SqlBulkCopy`, `NEXT VALUE FOR`, `ON COMMIT DROP`, `CREATE TEMPORARY SEQUENCE`, `dbo.`, `NVARCHAR`, `ISNULL(`, `GETDATE()`, `OPTION (MAXDOP`, `TOP N`, `@@IDENTITY`, `WITH (NOLOCK)`, `[bracketed]` identifiers, `#tempTable`; OR csproj references `DotNetCore.GaussDB`, `DotNetCore.EntityFrameworkCore.GaussDB`, `HuaweiCloud.Driver.GaussDB`, `HuaweiCloud.EntityFrameworkCore.GaussDB`, `Npgsql.EntityFramewor… ([binrogithub/1-3-Cloud-Adoption-Skills](https://github.com/binrogithub/1-3-Cloud-Adoption-Skills/tree/HEAD/Application-Modernization/Database-Migration/Relational-Database-Migration/GaussDB-Adaptation-Skill) · ★14 · 2 copies)
- **gaussdb-expert**: Expert database specialist focusing on GaussDB OLTP — Huawei's self-developed enterprise-grade relational database (NOT GaussDB(DWS) OLAP, NOT GaussDB(for openGauss) cloud service, NOT GaussDB(for MySQL)). Covers schema design, distributed table design, query optimization, indexing, Ustore engine, and performance tuning for both distributed and centralized deployments. ([amirhamzakhan2001/fehm](https://github.com/amirhamzakhan2001/fehm/tree/HEAD/skills/databases/gaussdb-expert))
- **gaussian-affinity-estimation**: Use when you have a high-dimensional data matrix (e.g. mnist2500_X.txt) plus data/null 2D t-SNE or UMAP embeddings and need a local, per-cell measure of how faithfully each cell's neighborhood survives embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-affinity-estimation))
- **gaussian-convolution-imputation-for-sparse-contact-maps**: Use when raw single-cell Hi-C contact matrices (N*N dat_HiC) are too sparse for differential testing and a bias-aware normalized count matrix is needed before scHiCDiff.KS/CVM/NB/ZINB detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-convolution-imputation-for-sparse-contact-maps))
- **gaussian-coordinate-perturbation**: Use when generating pseudo-paired spatial transcriptome / MALDI-MSI benchmark pairs from a single source tissue — e.g. to stress-test coordinate-alignment methods (manual gross, ICP 3D, direct alignment) against realistic inter-section morphology drift. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-coordinate-perturbation))
- **gaussian-copula-bivariate-emission-construction**: Use when comparing two ChIP-seq samples of broad histone marks (e.g. H3K27me3/H3K9me3) and you need the bivariate emission density behind histoneHMM's three-state differential classifications from binned bivariate read counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-copula-bivariate-emission-construction))
- **gaussian-covariance-sampling**: Use when you need a synthetic TT/TE/EE (or lensing) band-power data vector consistent with a candl likelihood's covariance — e.g. for forecasting, Fisher/bin-width optimisation studies, or validating a likelihood via chi-squared at the fiducial cosmology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/gaussian-covariance-sampling))
- **gaussian-curve-fitting-similarity-scoring**: Use when detected LC/MS chromatographic peaks must be quality-filtered by shape symmetry, e.g. peaks that look Gaussian-like versus zigzag, distorted, or asymmetric profiles after peak detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-curve-fitting-similarity-scoring))
- **gaussian-executor**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/gaussian-executor) · ★745)
- **gaussian-exponential-zero-noise-extrapolation**: Hybrid Gaussian-exponential zero-noise extrapolation methodology for periodic quantum circuits — combines Gaussian and exponential error models for more accurate expectation value estimation in NISQ-era quantum computing. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/quantum/gaussian-exponential-zero-noise-extrapolation) · ★4)
- **gaussian-filter-signal-smoothing**: Use when you have raw LC-MS ROI intensity arrays (m/z + intensity columns from a mass trace) and need robust chromatographic peak segmentation: smoothing precedes edge detection, never final quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-filter-signal-smoothing))
- **gaussian-fisher-superadditivity**: Use when measuring multiple Gaussian quantum modes jointly. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/gaussian-fisher-superadditivity) · ★4)
- **gaussian-graphical-model-data-simulation**: Use when evaluating a kernel or pathway-graph association test via Monte Carlo — you need synthetic correlated data whose covariance encodes a pathway graph under null or alternative hypotheses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-graphical-model-data-simulation))
- **gaussian-graphical-model-inference-large-scale**: Use when a log-normalized single-cell gene expression matrix with &gt;5000 cells needs partial-correlation (GGM) co-expression network inference, or when benchmarking SingleCellGGM against pcor-based methods like ppcor and SILGGM on an identical input matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-graphical-model-inference-large-scale))
- **gaussian-graphical-model-network-estimation**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics feature table (raw peak intensities) has two condition groups with features outnumbering samples (p &gt;&gt; n) and you need regularized metabolite-metabolite partial correlation networks rather than pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-graphical-model-network-estimation))
- **gaussian-graphical-model-penalized-estimation**: Use when a single-cell read-count matrix must be turned into a sparse, undirected gene co-expression network that separates direct gene-gene dependencies from indirect ones, and standard Pearson/Spearman correlations plus plain glasso are unreliable due to excessive zero counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-graphical-model-penalized-estimation))
- **gaussian-graphical-model-resampling**: Use when estimating partial-correlation metabolite-metabolite networks under p &gt;&gt; n with DNEA, after BIC tuning of lambda, and before getNetworks(), to stabilize edge inclusion via resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-graphical-model-resampling))
- **gaussian-graphical-model-simulation**: Use when benchmarking gene co-expression network inference methods on synthetic data where the ground-truth adjacency matrix must be known, especially for sparse scRNA-seq-like data with simulated dropouts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-graphical-model-simulation))
- **gaussian-grpo**: Gaussian Group Relative Policy Optimization (G²RPO) for multimodal RL training. Replaces linear scaling with distributional matching to ensure gradient equity across diverse tasks. Use when training multimodal models, balancing perception vs reasoning, or stabilizing RL across heterogeneous reward topologies. Keywords: G²RPO, Gaussian GRPO, multimodal RL, entropy shaping, response length shaping, GRPO, reinforcement learning. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/multi-agent-rl/gaussian-grpo) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **gaussian-hmm-baum-welch-training**: Use when a single-cell WGS analysis must pick a data-driven bin size before segmentation: specifically, when gHET calls phased by SHAPEIT2 are available and the amplification assay's ADO length distribution must be measured to set the minimum resolvable CNA bin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-hmm-baum-welch-training))
- **gaussian-kernel-affinity-computation**: Use when a single-cell gene expression matrix (N cells x D genes) must be converted into a probabilistic neighborhood graph or Markov transition matrix as the substrate for diffusion/random-walk based analyses such as RWR, diffusion pseudotime, or trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-kernel-affinity-computation))
- **gaussian-kernel-bandwidth-calibration**: Use when fitting a semi-parametric kernel machine regression g(Y)=Xβ+h(Z)+ϵ on pathway metabolite features and need a Gaussian kernel whose bandwidth is calibrated from the data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-kernel-bandwidth-calibration))
- **gaussian-kernel-nearest-neighbor-graph**: Use when a single-cell expression matrix (e.g. 438 cells x 48 genes) must be converted into a Markov transition matrix as input to diffusion-based manifold learning such as BKFD or diffusion pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-kernel-nearest-neighbor-graph))
- **gaussian-kernel-reference-distribution-on-grid**: Use when computing per-gene differential expression significance from cell positions in a multidimensional space (t-SNE, PCA, spatial or pseudotime coordinates) without clustering cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-kernel-reference-distribution-on-grid))
- **gaussian-kernel-regression-agreement-fitting**: Use when you have matched per-target log2 peak-area tables from two integration methods (e.g., an open-source R pipeline vs. vendor software such as Xcalibur) and need to quantify whether the automated tool reproduces expert manual integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-kernel-regression-agreement-fitting))
- **gaussian-kernel-spatial-smoothing**: Use when you have ST spot spatial coordinates (e.g. simu_st_metadata.csv from the Figshare deposit DOI 10.6084/m9.figshare.24452812) and need the normalized adjacency matrix that serves as STEM's prediction goal for spot-level spatial relationships. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-kernel-spatial-smoothing))
- **gaussian-likelihood-scoring-of-clusters**: Use when spatial transcriptomics clusterings must be ranked without ground-truth labels, e.g. to pick the alpha/beta configuration by the goodness score v(y,Cα) instead of ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-likelihood-scoring-of-clusters))
- **gaussian-mean-width-identification-capacity**: Single-letter efficiently computable strong converse bound on classical identification capacity of quantum channels via Gaussian mean width analysis ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/general-ml/gaussian-mean-width-identification-capacity) · ★4)
- **gaussian-mixture-ambient-noise-modeling**: Use when normalizing droplet-based protein (ADT) counts from CITE-seq/ASAP-seq/TEA-seq where ambient unbound-antibody noise and cell-level technical noise must be removed before clustering or differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-ambient-noise-modeling))
- **gaussian-mixture-cell-classification**: Use when a labeled reference scRNA-seq dataset with cluster-specific gene signatures and weights (e.g., from scID_weight) is available and unlabeled target cells must be assigned to reference clusters from per-cell signature scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-cell-classification))
- **gaussian-mixture-clustering-cnv**: Use when binned scATAC-seq accessibility data must be decomposed into per-cell copy-number states using Gaussian mixture modeling, e.g. with Copy-scAT's clusterCNV/Mclust after collapseChrom3N arm-level summarization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-clustering-cnv))
- **gaussian-mixture-clustering-of-surface-protein-markers**: Use when CITE-seq data provides surface-protein ADT levels for markers (e.g., CD16/CD56) whose RNA is dropout-obscured, and you need protein-derived sub-population labels to quantify or benchmark RNA-based (or denoised) recovery of that structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-clustering-of-surface-protein-markers))
- **gaussian-mixture-denoising-of-adt-data**: Use when normalizing droplet-based protein (ADT) data from CITE-seq, ASAP-seq, or TEA-seq where ambient antibody noise and cell-to-cell technical variation confound protein values and raw/CLR values track empty-droplet signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-denoising-of-adt-data))
- **gaussian-mixture-density-decomposition**: Use when a population of per-cell confidence scores (e.g., Wilcoxon rank-sum scores on marker genes) must be split into validated vs. unvalidated sub-populations without an arbitrary hard cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-density-decomposition))
- **gaussian-mixture-em-decoupling**: Use when analyzing scCRISPR-seq cell-by-gene matrices where perturbation (Y) and control (Z) groups have differing cluster proportions, so naive fold-change estimation is distorted by infected proportion bias and the true cellular response must be decoupled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-em-decoupling))
- **gaussian-mixture-em-fitting**: Use when quantifying extranuclear RNA contamination per droplet in droplet-based snRNA-seq: the percent of reads spliced is bimodal with nuclear and background droplets straddling ~50%, and a sample-specific midpoint separating the two distributions must be estimated per experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-em-fitting))
- **gaussian-mixture-expectation-maximization-estimation**: Use when performing differential abundance analysis of compositional microbiome data where differences in sampling fractions across samples introduce bias that must be estimated from the data itself. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-expectation-maximization-estimation))
- **gaussian-mixture-fitting-of-hashtag-distributions**: Use when demultiplexing cell hashing experiments and per-hashtag cutoffs must distinguish true positive from background signal, especially with many hashtags or imbalanced sample sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-fitting-of-hashtag-distributions))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-gene-expression-pcs**: Use when expert-provided scribble annotations are unavailable and you need an initial non-spatial domain labeling of Visium or ST spots from principal components of raw gene expression as input to automated scribble generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-gene-expression-pcs))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-single-cell-transcriptomes**: Use when you need to partition unsynchronized single-cell transcriptome data (e.g. log2(TPM+1) cycle-base-gene expression) into a user-defined number of clusters k before reconstructing a pseudo time-series. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-clustering-of-single-cell-transcriptomes))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-em-estimation**: Use when scCRISPR-seq perturbation vs control groups differ in cluster proportions (infected proportion bias) and you need per-gRNA cellular responses decoupled from that bias on selected infection-biased PCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-em-estimation))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-estimation**: Use when fitting a calibration curve from calibration-standard injections (per-feature peak_area from a FeatureDB/mzML report) for targeted LC-MS/MS quantitation where a generic linear fit is insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-estimation))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-fitting**: Use when a user-supplied CSV gene-expression data frame (gene identifiers plus log-fold-change values) must be partitioned into distinct expression-pattern clusters using unsupervised, probability-based Gaussian mixture modelling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-fitting--6d928929dbd1e6ca))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-fitting**: Use when assigning gRNAs to cells in a single-cell CRISPR screen and per-guide UMI count distributions must be split into endogenous vs ambient components without a user-defined fixed UMI threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-fitting--f2ab6b3b35254e00))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-fitting-per-cell**: Use when normalizing CITE-seq/ASAP-seq/TEA-seq ADT data with dsb step II, or when validating that k=2 Gaussian mixtures fit per-cell protein distributions via BIC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-fitting-per-cell))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-initialization**: Use when fitting a LUCID model on exposures G, omics Z (possibly with sporadic or list-wise missing values), and outcome Y, and you need deterministic starting values for the EM algorithm over K latent clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-initialization))
- **gaussian-mixture-model-subclone-separability**: Use when comparing how accurately different cell-cycle correction methods (Cyclum, Seurat, ccRemover, PC1–5) preserve or recover subclone structure in scRNA-seq data containing two known subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-model-subclone-separability))
- **gaussian-mixture-posterior-thresholding**: Use when continuous numeric trait columns (e.g., yogurt metabolite concentrations) must be converted to binary case/control labels for Scoary2 association testing and uncertain isolates need a principled exclusion rule. - **gaussian-mixture-prior-initialization**: Use when implementing or reproducing SCALE's VAE+GMM architecture on scATAC-seq data (e.g. Forebrain.h5ad peak-by-barcode matrix) and a tight posterior estimate over latent features is needed for clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gaussian-mixture-prior-initialization))

- **gaussian-mixture-reliability-modeling**: Use when transferring labels from annotated scRNA-seq to unlabeled scATAC-seq and you must decide per-cell which target cells can safely be merged into the annotated source during iterative integration. - **gaussian-mixture-technical-component-denoising**: Use when ADT/protein counts have already been ambient-corrected against empty droplets and residual cell-to-cell technical noise (correlated with isotype controls and background mean) must be regressed out before clustering or UMAP. - **gaussian-mixture-umi-threshold-fitting**: Use when assigning gRNAs to cells in single-cell CRISPR screens whose per-gRNA UMI distributions vary widely (low vs high UMI experiments), where a fixed min_umi threshold over- or under-assigns cells; triggered by UMI-histogram bimodality or inter-experiment differences in average gRNA UMIs. - **gaussian-noise-perturbation-of-scrnaseq-data**: Use when you need to quantify the noise robustness of a single-cell trajectory inference or alignment pipeline by sweeping Gaussian noise levels on synthetic scRNA-seq data and recording performance-metric distributions per noise level.
